Alternative: Use generic nonconvex optimization but with smart initialization. Projected gradient for max with random restarts may find good. Let's outline algorithm that likely works:
- Work with p sum1 nonnegative.
- Compute C, max_m, objective=2n*m.
- Gradient of C_k = 2 p_{k-i} as above.
- Use aggregate subgradient q over top coefficients: choose q_k ∝ C_k^η among active (C_k > m - tol). Then gradient = 2 ∑ q_k p_{k-i}. This measures a weighted "reversed" window. Then update p by reducing where gradient high (since increasing p_i increases those coefficients). For minimizing max, we want p_i ∝ exp(-∑ λ_i grad_i)? But normalized nonneg. Simple multiplicative update:
   w_i = p_i * exp(-η * grad_i / scale)
   p = w / sum(w)
This is like Mirror descent (softmax) on simplex for nonsmooth max. Nonnegative, efficient. Could use line search/backtracking reducing objective. This might avoid zeros. Also active q maybe uniform top.
- Also do anti-symmetric initial and damping.

Could implement a robust local optimizer:
function local_search(p, time_limit): 
  n=len(p); p normalized.

Let's design full sequence local search algorithm in detail. We can run multiple independent workers (sequential) with different init/time.

Initialization candidates for final n (e.g., n=8192):
- Provided height_sequence_1 if length maybe 4498; we can resample to n, normalize, apply taper/cosine? Could also use as initial after local perturb.
- Cosine family p_i ∝ exp(Σ_{m=1}^M a_m cos(mπx_i) + b_m sin(mπx_i)). Need generate random coefficient sets. We can search coefficients by simpler random + local search. For each init, run local_search with time_budget. Keep best.

Local_search function:
Input p (np float length n), time_limit, seed.
- Normalize p sum1, clip small.
- best_score = score_fft(p), best_p.
- Set current p.
- Maybe precompute x_i for basis? Not needed for full.
- Loop while time < limit:
   - Compute C = conv_fft(p, L) and m=max(C), score=2n*m. If score better, update.
   - Active weights q. Need choose. Strategies/adapt:
       * If m <=0? not.
       * Determine threshold = m - tol*m - tol_abs. tol maybe 0.02 or dynamic. Use indices where C >= threshold.
       * If active count too small (<16), include top min(64, //). If active count too large (>2n/4), use top fraction.
       * q = 1/active_count uniform over active, or weighted by (C/m)^ρ, e.g., C**(2 or 8). But if threshold includes near-max, weights. Use softmax(C/τ) with τ = max(m_active/κ, min). Perhaps simpler: q = np.zeros(2n-1); C_sel = C[C>=threshold]; weights = C_sel ** power; normalize. Power parameter; can randomize. Weighted focusing more maximum.

def local_adam(p0, time_limit, rng, start_lr=0.15, anneal_start=5.0, anneal_end=30.0, max_iters=None):
   p = normalize(p0)
   n=p.size; L=next_power_of_two(2*n)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
   best=p.copy(); best_score=2*n*conv_fft(p,L).max()
   m_adam=np.zeros(n); v_adam=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=start_lr; no_improve=0; stagnation=50
   eta=anneal_start
   base_lr=start_lr
   while time...:
      p=softmax(z)
      C=conv_fft(p,L); m=C.max(); score=2*n*m
      if score < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0; maybe update eta schedule
      else: no_improve+=1
      if no_improve > stagnation:
          # perturb z and/or restart from best
          if rng.random()<0.5:
             z = np.log(best) - random noise
          else:
             z += (rng.random(n)-0.5)*lr*2
          no_improve=0; reduce? 
      # eta schedule: eta = start + (end-start)*progress or based on no_improve
      progress = min(1.0, t / 2000.0)
      eta = anneal_start + (anneal_end - anneal_start)*progress
      tau = m/eta if m>0 else 1e-12
      # weights
      zq = C - m  # <=0
      # To avoid under/overflow, use exp(zq/tau)
      w = np.exp(zq/tau)  # w[active]=1, others small; for tau small many 0
      s = w.sum(); if not finite or s<1e-300: use active uniform top.
      q = w/s
      # gradient p: g = cross_corr(p,q,L) (without factor 2)
      P = np.fft.rfft(p, L) (we already have? We can recompute or keep P from C? C computation used P; can return P. Modify conv_fft to return C only; for grad, recompute P to simplify. But double FFT. Could write compute_C_and_P.
      P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
      C = irfft(P*P)
      Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
      gp = np.fft.irfft(P.conj()*Q, L)[:n]
      # gp = ∑q p; derivative C=2gp, so use g=2*gp.
      # gradient wrt z: grad_z = p*g - p*mean(p*g)
      v = p*gp (since factor2 cancels in normalize?) Use gp or 2gp. Scale doesn't affect Adam sign. Use gp.
      avg = np.dot(p, gp)
      grad_z = p*gp - p*avg
      # Adam update
      t+=1; b1,b2=0.9,0.999; bc1=1-b1**t; bc2=1-b2**t
      m_adam = b1*m_adam + (1-b1)*grad_z
      v_adam = b2*v_adam + (1-b2)*grad_z*grad_z
      step = lr * (m_adam/bc1) / (np.sqrt(v_adam/bc2)+1e-8)
      # optionally clip step to [-max_step,max_step]
      step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
      # line search over multipliers [1,0.5,0.25,0.1]
      z_trial = z - step*mult
      p_trial=softmax(z_trial); score_trial=2*n*conv_fft(p_trial,L).max()
      choose mult with best score; if best_trial < current_score*0.999? accept; else maybe accept if not worse by much? To progress smooth objective, actual score might stagnate but smooth improves. We can track F_soft = m + tau logsumexp? Could accept if soft objective lower, even if actual max not yet lower. Need compute soft objective (same C): F = m + tau*log(s) where s=sum(exp((C-m)/tau)). We can use F for line search. At early anneal, F smooth. At end, actual.
      accept if F_trial < F_current - 1e-12 or score_trial<best? We'll compute F for current and trial.
      if accept: z=z_trial; if no_improve? no_improve reset when best improves; else maybe still.
      else: lr = max(lr*0.7, base_lr*0.05); after repeated fail, increase? maybe.
      # periodic recenter z: z-=median? mean.
      # occasionally add high-frequency perturbation if no improve.
   return best,best_score.

Let's flesh local_adam code carefully:

def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    zm = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(zm)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        s = 1.0; e = np.ones_like(zm)/z.size
    return e/s

def normalize_nonneg(x):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
   x[x<0]=0
   s=x.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full(len,1/len)
   return x/s

def convolution_score(p, L=None):
   n=p.size
   if L is None: L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)
   C=C[:2*n-1]
   # numerical imaginary? irfft real
   m = float(C.max())
   score = 2.0*n*m
   return score, C, m, P maybe. But returning P helps.

In local_adam, current computation can return C,m,P, and F:
def soft_objective(C, m, eta):
   if m <=0: return 0, ones?
   tau=m/eta
   if tau <=0: tau=1e-300
   z=C-m
   # To avoid underflow, normalize by max z=0; exp zeros for low.
   # If tau very small, z/tau can be -inf for z<0; okay.
   w=np.exp(z/tau)
   s=w.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0:
      # active uniform top 8
      top=min(8,C.size); idx=np.argpartition(C, -top)[-top:]; w[idx]=1; s=top
   F=m + tau*np.log(s)
   q=w/s (if s finite)
   return F,q,m

If C has negative tiny due FFT roundoff, clip C=np.maximum(C,0). m max.

Let's implement local_adam_basis:
def local_adam_basis(n, M, theta0, time_limit, rng, base_lr=0.2):
   x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
   B = build basis array shape n x 2M.
   ztheta=theta0.copy()
   best_p=None,best_score=inf
   m_theta=np.zeros(D), v_theta; t=0; lr=base*sqrt? no.
   eta schedule.
   while time:
      logp = B @ ztheta
      p=softmax(logp)
      compute C,m, P? C via conv_fft(p). F,q.
      g= cross_corr(p,q) (need P and Q; we can recompute P)
      pg = p*g
      dotp = gp? = np.dot(p,g)
      gradθ = B.T @ pg - dotp * B.T @ p
      Adam update.
      line search multipliers on θ.
      accept if F improves and score not too worse.
   return best.

def local_adam(p0, budget_s, rng, base_lr=0.25, eta_start=4.0, eta_end=40.0):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2: return p, inf
    L = next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = 2*n*conv_max_fft(p,L)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z = z - np.mean(z)
    m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=base_lr; max_step=1.0
    no_improve=0; start_time=time.monotonic(); deadline=start_time+budget_s
    last_best_time=start_time
    best_F=inf? track.
    fail_count=0
    iteration=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = softmax_from_z(z)
        # maybe re-normalize p exactly
        s=p.sum(); if s<=0 or not finite: reinit
        P = np.fft.rfft(p,L)
        C = np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
        C = np.maximum(C,0.0)
        m = float(C.max())
        score = 2.0*n*m
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score; best = p.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0; fail_count=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        for best if score<best score? yes.
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start_time)/max(1.0,budget_s*0.8))
        eta = eta_start + (eta_end-eta_start)*progress
        F_current, q = soft_F_and_q(C,m,eta)
        Q = np.fft.rfft(q,L)
        gp = np.fft.irfft(P.conj()*Q,L)[:n]
        gp = np.maximum(gp,0.0)
        if not finite gp: ...
        max_gp = gp.max()
        if max_gp <= 1e-300 or not finite:
            # random perturb
        else:
            avg = float(np.dot(p,gp))
            grad_z = p*gp - p*avg
            # if very small, perturb
            t +=1
            b1=0.9;b2=0.999; cb1=1-b1**t; cb2=1-b2**t
            m1 = b1*m1 + (1-b1)*grad_z
            v2 = b2*v2 + (1-b2)*(grad_z*grad_z)
            update = -lr * (m1/cb1)/(np.sqrt(v2/cb2)+1e-8)
            max_upd = np.abs(update).max()
            if max_upd > max_step: update = update * (max_step/max_upd)
            # line search
            accepted=False
            for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
                z_trial = z + mult*update
                # optional clip range
                if z_trial.max()-z_trial.min() > 50: z_trial = np.clip(z_trial - np.mean(z_trial), -30,30)
                p_trial = softmax_from_z(z_trial)
                sc_trial, F_trial = score_and_F(p_trial,L,eta)
                if np.isfinite(F_trial) and F_trial <= F_current + 1e-12 and sc_trial <= score + max(0.02, 0.1*score):
                    # accept if F improved significantly or score improved
                    if F_trial < F_current - 1e-14 or sc_trial < score - 1e-12:
                        z = z_trial; accepted=True; fail_count=0; break
            if not accepted:
                lr = max(lr*0.7, base_lr*0.05)
                fail_count +=1
                if fail_count > 8:
                    # perturbation
                    sigma = 0.2
                    if rng.random()<0.3:
                        # mix best plus noise
                        z = np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z-=np.mean(z)
                    z += (rng.random(n)-0.5)*sigma
                    z -= np.mean(z)
                    m1.fill(0); v2.fill(0); t=0; lr=base_lr; fail_count=0
                    no_improve=0
        # If no improvement for long and fail_count low, do smooth perturbation
        if no_improve > 200:
            sigma = 0.3
            # random smooth via FFT? simple gaussian
            z = z + (rng.random(n)-0.5)*sigma
            z -= np.mean(z)
            m1.fill(0); v2.fill(0); t=0; lr=max(lr,base_lr*0.5); no_improve=0
        # long no best (time)
        if time.monotonic() - last_best_time > max(60.0, 0.2*budget_s):
            # more aggressive restart from random/best
            if rng.random()<0.5:
               z = np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
            else:
               z = random_smooth_z(rng,n, amplitude=1.0)
            z = z - np.mean(z)
            m1.fill(0);v2.fill(0);t=0;lr=base_lr;no_improve=0
        # limit eps
        if p has nonfinite: reinit from best
    return best,best_score

def score_and_soft(p,L,eta):
   P=rfft(p,L); C=irfft; C max; m; F=m + m/eta*logsumexp(C-m) (using stable). Return score,F.

random_smooth_z function:
def random_smooth_z(rng,n,amplitude=0.8,M=16):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for m in 1..M:
      a=rng.normal(0, amplitude/m)
      b=rng.normal(0, amplitude/m)
      z += a*np.cos(np.pi*m*x) + b*np.sin(np.pi*m*x)
   return z

next_pow2:
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return int(2 ** (m-1).bit_length())

normalize_nonneg:
def normalize_nonneg(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim !=1 or x.size==0: return np.ones(32)/32
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s=float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0: return np.full(x.size, 1.0/x.size)
    return x/s

softmax_from_log:
def softmax_from_log(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z=z - float(np.mean(z)) # optional? For stability, mean; if z large.
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    z=z - zmax
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.full(z.size,1.0/z.size)
    return e/s
Note: Subtract mean then max; subtract mean after max okay. For basis logp, subtract mean? Softmax invariant; subtracting mean changes log values but same p. Good. However local_adam uses z separately; if softmax subtracts mean, p invariant. But z in local still original; fine.
Potential: If z has huge range, after z = z - mean, if max still huge >700, exp(z-max) safe. If z contains nan, set 0.

conv_fft:
def convolution_fft(p,L=None):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=p.size
   if L is None: L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   return c
Might allocate; okay.

score_of_p:
def score_of_p(p,L=None):
   C=convolution_fft(p,L)
   m=C.max() if C.size else 0
   return 2.0*p.size*float(m), m
For internal many, avoid separate.

soft_F_and_q:
def soft_F_and_q(C,m,eta):
   Lc=C.size
   if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
       q=np.full(Lc,1.0/Lc); return 0.0,q
   tau=m/eta
   if tau <= 0: tau=1e-300
   # for stability, if tau extremely small, use active top
   if tau < m*1e-6:
       # top 32
       if Lc <=32: idx=np.arange(Lc)
       else: idx=np.argpartition(C, -32)[-32:]
       w=np.zeros(Lc); w[idx]=1.0; s=float(idx.size); F=m
       q=w/s; return F,q
   z=(C-m)/tau
   # z max 0, min maybe -large. clip to -50 to avoid underflow? exp(-1000)=0 anyway, but clip for speed? okay.
   z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)  # wait if tau large, most z near0; clipping at -50 corresponds weight <2e-22, okay. But if C range small and important weights 1e-20? Not needed.
   w=np.exp(z)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
       idx=np.argpartition(C, -min(32,Lc))[-min(32,Lc):]
       w=np.zeros(Lc); w[idx]=1.0; s=float(idx.size)
   q=w/s
   F=m + tau*float(np.log(s))
   return F,q
Clipping z at -50 may degrade smooth objective when tau big? If tau=m/eta, eta>=4, z min=-eta (since C min0) = -4 to -40. So -50 safe. For eta 40, z min -40, safe. Good.

trial_score_F:
def trial_score_F(p,L,eta):
   C=convolution_fft(p,L); m=C.max(); score=2*n*m; F,_ = soft_F_and_q(C,m,eta); return score,F

random_smooth_z:
def random_smooth_z(rng,n,scale=0.3):
    # use random Fourier coefficients
    z=np.zeros(n)
    freqs=np.arange(1, min(32,n)+1)
    # generate noise in freq domain and transform
    # Simpler: white + low-pass?
    # Use np.fft.irfft(random cos/sin)
    L=next_pow2(n)
    spec=np.zeros(L//2+1, complex)
    spec[0]=0
    # random amplitudes decay
    amp = np.exp(-freqs/10.0) * scale
    phase = 2*np.pi*rng.random(len(freqs))
    vals = amp*np.exp(1j*phase)
    # place positive/negative? for real, set both? Easier: use sum of sine waves.
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for k,a,ph in zip(freqs,amp,phase): z += a*np.cos(np.pi*k*x+ph)
    return z

resample_log:
def resample_log(p,n_new):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); n_old=p.size
   if n_old==0: return ones
   logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   logp -= np.mean(logp)
   if n_old == n_new: out=logp
   else:
      x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n_old)
      x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
      out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,logp)
   p_new=softmax_from_log(out)
   return p_new

local_adam implementation details with time. Need avoid re-initializing z if p all zeros.

Pseudo code final:

def local_adam(p0,budget_s,rng,base_lr=0.25,eta_start=4.0,eta_end=40.0):
    start=np.asarray(p0,float)
    if start.size ==0: return None, inf
    if start.size < 8: start = resample_log(start,32)? maybe not.
    p=normalize_nonneg(start)
    n=p.size
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=p.copy()
    C=convolution_fft(p,L); m=C.max(); best_score=2*n*m
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=np.mean(z)
    m1=np.zeros(n); v2=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=base_lr; fail=0; no_improve=0; last_best_time=time.monotonic(); deadline=start_time+budget_s
    best_score_val=best_score
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       p=softmax_from_log(z)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       C=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
       C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
       m=float(C.max())
       score=2*n*m
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best=p.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0; fail=0
       else: no_improve += 1
       progress=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-start_time)/max(1.0,budget_s))
       eta=eta_start + (eta_end-eta_start)*progress
       F,q=soft_F_and_q(C,m,eta)
       Q=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
       gp=np.fft.irfft(P.conj()*Q,L)[:n]
       np.maximum(gp,0.0,out=gp)?? np.maximum(gp,0,gp)
       if not finite or gp.max()==0:
           z += random_smooth?; reset; continue
       avg=float(np.dot(p,gp))
       grad_z=p*gp - p*avg
       # if grad_z std small, perturb
       if np.abs(grad_z).max() <= 1e-300:
           z = np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z-=np.mean(z); z += rng.normal(0,0.1,n); reset
           continue
       t += 1
       b1=0.9;b2=0.999; cb1=1-b1**t; cb2=1-b2**t
       m1=b1*m1 + (1-b1)*grad_z
       v2=b2*v2 + (1-b2)*grad_z*grad_z
       update = -lr * (m1/cb1) / (np.sqrt(v2/cb2)+1e-8)
       maxu=float(np.max(np.abs(update)))
       max_step=1.2
       if maxu > max_step: update *= max_step/maxu
       accepted=False
       current_score=score
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
          zt=z + mult*update
          if zt.max()-zt.min() > 60: zt=np.clip(zt-np.mean(zt),-40,40)
          pt=softmax_from_log(zt)
          sc,Ft=trial_score_F(pt,L,eta)
          if np.isfinite(Ft) and (Ft < F - 1e-14 or sc < best_score - 1e-12) and sc <= current_score + max(0.05,0.1*current_score):
             z=zt; accepted=True; fail=0; break
       if not accepted:
          lr = max(lr*0.75, base_lr*0.05)
          fail +=1
          if fail > 10:
             # restart
             if rng.random()<0.5:
                z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); 
             else:
                z=random_smooth_z(rng,n,1.0)
             z-=np.mean(z)
             m1.fill(0); v2.fill(0); t=0; lr=base_lr; fail=0; no_improve=0
       if no_improve > 200:
          z += rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
          z-=np.mean(z); m1.fill(0); v2.fill(0); t=0; no_improve=0
       if time.monotonic()-last_best_time > max(120.0,0.25*budget_s):
          if rng.random()<0.5:
             z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); 
          else:
             z=random_smooth_z(rng,n,1.2)
          z-=np.mean(z); m1.fill(0); v2.fill(0); t=0; lr=base_lr; no_improve=0
    return best,best_score

local_adam_basis code:
def local_adam_basis(n, M, theta0, budget_s, rng, base_lr=0.1):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # build B as list? Use array
   if M<=0: return softmax_from_log(np.zeros(n)), inf
   cols=[]
   for k in range(1,M+1):
       cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*k*x))
       cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*k*x))
   B=np.column_stack(cols)
   D=B.shape[1]
   theta=np.asarray(theta0,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
   if theta.size<D: pad zeros; else take first.
   theta=np.clip(theta,-5,5)
   L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   best_p=None; best_score=inf
   m1=np.zeros(D);v2=np.zeros(D);t=0;lr=base_lr; fail=0; no_improve=0; last_best=...
   start_time...
   while time:
      logp=B@theta
      p=softmax_from_log(logp)
      P=rfft; C; m; score...
      update best
      F,q=soft_F
      Q=fft(q); gp=irfft; max(0)
      pg=p*gp; dotp=np.dot(p,gp); Et = B.T@p
      grad = B.T@pg - dotp*Et
      # scale grad? if large, clip
      maxg=np.abs(grad).max(); if >0: grad /= maxg? Adam normalization cancels but if some large, okay. Could normalize to avoid Adam eps issues.
      t +=1; Adam; update = -lr*...
      max_step=1.0; clip.
      line search theta_trial=theta+mult*update; clip [-6,6]; p_trial=softmax(B@theta_trial); sc,Ft=...
      accept if Ft < F - tol or sc improvements.
      if not accepted fail; if fail>10 reset theta random or from best_p projected? We can project best_p to basis:
         zbest=np.log(np.maximum(best_p,1e-300)); theta_best=np.linalg.lstsq(B,zbest,rcond=None)[0]; theta=theta_best + small random.
      long no best similar.
   return best_p,best_score

LP function:
def lp_improve(p0, budget_s, rng=None):
   try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
   except Exception: return normalize(p0), score
   p=normalize(p0); n=p.size
   # If n > 256, downsample? Use n as provided. We'll call with n=128.
   deadline...
   best=p; best_score=score
   t_list=[0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]
   stall=0
   while time<deadline:
      C=conv; m=max
      if m<=0 break
      # build A
      K=np.arange(2*n-1)[:,None]
      I=np.arange(n)[None,:]
      J=K-I
      mask=(J>=0)&(J<n)
      Jsafe=np.where(mask,J,0)
      A=np.where(mask,p[Jsafe],0.0)
      res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=m*np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
      if not res.success: break
      g=np.asarray(res.x,float); g=np.maximum(g,0); s=float(g.sum())
      if s <=1.0000005: break
      gn=g/s
      improved=False
      for tt in t_list:
         pn=(1-tt)*p + tt*gn
         pn=normalize_nonneg(pn)
         sc=score...
         if sc < best_score -1e-12:
             best_p=pn; best_score=sc; p=pn; improved=True; break
      if improved: stall=0
      else:
         stall+=1
         if stall > 20:
             # perturb
             p = normalize_nonneg(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,n)))
             stall=0
   return best,best_score
```

def make_cos_p(n, amp, mode=1, phase=0.0):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=amp*np.cos(mode*np.pi*x+phase)
   return softmax_from_log(z)
def make_smooth_random_p(n,rng,scale=1.0):
   z=random_smooth_z(rng,n,scale)
   return softmax_from_log(z)

if cand_time > 60 and cand_score_list:
   per=cand_time/len(cand_score_list)
   for sc,p in cand_score_list:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline-60: break
      alloc = min(per, deadline-60-time.monotonic())
      if alloc < 30: break
      p_out,s_out = local_adam(p, alloc, rng, base_lr=0.25)
      update
# polish
if time.monotonic() < deadline-20:
   p_in = best_seq if best_seq is not None else make_cos_p(final_n,0.6)
   if p_in.size != final_n:
       p_in = resample_log(p_in, final_n)
   p_out,s_out = local_adam(p_in, deadline-25-time.monotonic(), rng, base_lr=0.25)
   update
```

```python
"""
Search for low-maximim linear-convolution nonnegative sequences.

The objective is homogeneous, so internally we work with probability vectors
p = a / sum(a), sum(p) = 1, and minimize 2*n*max(p*p).

The main search is a smoothed maximum-subgradient / softmax mirror optimizer,
with Adam damping, line search, stochastic restarts, and multi-scale
initialization.  A small optional LP-based move is included when SciPy's
HiGHS solver is available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return int(2 ** (m - 1).bit_length())


def normalize_nonneg(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim != 1 or x.size == 0:
        x = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return x / s


def softmax_from_log(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zmax
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def convolution_fft(p, L=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([float(p[0]) ** 2 if n else 0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_of_p(p, L=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    C = convolution_fft(p, L)
    if C.size == 0:
        return np.inf, 0.0
    m = float(C.max())
    return float(2.0 * p.size * m), m


def soft_F_and_q(C, m, eta):
    Lc = C.size
    if C.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if (not np.isfinite(m)) or m <= 0.0:
        q = np.full(Lc, 1.0 / Lc, dtype=np.float64)
        return 0.0, q

    tau = m / max(1.0, eta)
    if tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1e-300

    if tau < m * 1e-6:
        k = min(32, Lc)
        if k <= 0:
            q = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
            return m, q
        idx = np.argpartition(C, Lc - k)[-k:]
        w = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        q = w / float(idx.size)
        return m, q

    zz = (C - m) / tau
    zz = np.clip(zz, -50.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(zz)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
        k = min(32, Lc)
        idx = np.argpartition(C, Lc - k)[-k:]
        w = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        s = float(idx.size)

    q = w / s
    F = m + tau * float(np.log(s))
    return F, q


def trial_score_F(p, L, eta):
    C = convolution_fft(p, L)
    m = float(C.max())
    score = float(2.0 * p.size * m)
    F, _ = soft_F_and_q(C, m, eta)
    return score, F


def random_smooth_z(rng, n, scale=0.35, max_modes=24):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    n_modes = min(max_modes, max(2, n // 2))
    for k in range(1, n_modes + 1):
        amp = scale / sqrt_float(k)
        phase = float(2.0 * np.pi * rng.random())
        z += amp * np.cos(np.pi * k * x + phase)
    return z


def sqrt_float(x):
    return float(np.sqrt(float(x)))


def make_cos_p(n, amp, mode=1, phase=0.0):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = float(amp) * np.cos(mode * np.pi * x + float(phase))
    return normalize_nonneg(softmax_from_log(z))


def make_smooth_random_p(n, rng, scale=0.75):
    z = random_smooth_z(rng, n, scale=scale)
    return normalize_nonneg(softmax_from_log(z))


def resample_log(p, n_new):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = normalize_nonneg(p)
    n_old = p.size
    if n_old == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n_new), dtype=np.float64) / int(n_new)
    if n_old == int(n_new):
        return p.copy()

    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    lp = lp - float(np.mean(lp))

    if n_old == 1:
        return softmax_from_log(np.zeros(int(n_new)))

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_new))
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, lp)
    return normalize_nonneg(softmax_from_log(out))


def local_adam(p0, budget_s, rng, base_lr=0.25, eta_start=4.0, eta_end=60.0):
    p = normalize_nonneg(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n < 2:
        return p, 2.0
    if (not np.isfinite(budget_s)) or budget_s <= 1.0:
        sc, _ = score_of_p(p)
        return p, sc

    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    C0 = convolution_fft(p, L)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * C0.max())
    best = p.copy()

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = base_lr
    fail = 0
    no_improve = 0
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + float(budget_s)
    last_best_time = start_time
    last_print = start_time

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = softmax_from_log(z)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = best.copy()
            z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            continue

        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        C = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
        m = float(C.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * m)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = float(score)
            best = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            last_best_time = time.monotonic()
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start_time) / max(1.0, float(budget_s)))
        eta = eta_start + (eta_end - eta_start) * progress
        F, q = soft_F_and_q(C, m, eta)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
            q = np.full(C.size, 1.0 / C.size)

        Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
        gp = np.fft.irfft(P.conj() * Q, L)[:n]
        gp = np.maximum(gp, 0.0)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gp)) or float(gp.max()) <= 1e-300:
            z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            z += (rng.random(n) - 0.5) * 0.35
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            fail = 0
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        avg = float(np.dot(p, gp))
        grad_z = p * gp - p * avg
        abs_max = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_z)))

        if abs_max <= 1e-300:
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            else:
                z = random_smooth_z(rng, n, scale=1.0)
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = base_lr
            fail = 0
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        t += 1
        cb1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
        cb2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t
        mom = b1 * mom + (1.0 - b1) * grad_z
        vel = b2 * vel + (1.0 - b2) * (grad_z * grad_z)

        update = -lr * (mom / cb1) / (np.sqrt(vel / cb2) + 1e-8)
        update = np.where(np.isfinite(update), update, 0.0)
        max_step = 1.2
        mu = float(np.max(np.abs(update)))
        if mu > max_step:
            update *= max_step / mu

        accepted = False
        ftol = 1e-11 * max(1.0, abs(F))
        score_tol = max(0.02, 0.05 * score)

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
            zt = z + mult * update
            span = float(zt.max() - zt.min())
            if span > 50.0:
                zt = np.clip(zt - float(np.mean(zt)), -35.0, 35.0)

            pt = softmax_from_log(zt)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(pt)):
                continue

            st, Ft = trial_score_F(pt, L, eta)
            if (not np.isfinite(st)) or (not np.isfinite(Ft)):
                continue

            if Ft <= F + ftol and st <= score + score_tol:
                if (Ft < F - ftol) or (st < best_score - 1e-12):
                    z = zt
                    accepted = True
                    fail = 0
                    break

        if not accepted:
            lr = max(lr * 0.72, base_lr * 0.04)
            fail += 1
            if fail >= 8:
                if rng.random() < 0.55:
                    z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
                else:
                    z = random_smooth_z(rng, n, scale=1.2)
                z = z - float(np.mean(z))
                mom.fill(0.0)
                vel.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                lr = base_lr
                fail = 0
                no_improve = 0

        if no_improve >= 180:
            z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)
            z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            no_improve = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_best_time > max(120.0, 0.25 * float(budget_s)):
            if rng.random() < 0.6:
                z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            else:
                z = random_smooth_z(rng, n, scale=1.5)
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = base_lr
            no_improve = 0
            last_best_time = time.monotonic()

        if time.monotonic() - last_print >= 45.0:
            print("local_adam n=%d score=%.6f lr=%.4f" % (n, best_score, lr), flush=True)
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def build_basis(n, M):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    cols = []
    M = int(M)
    for k in range(1, M + 1):
        cols.append(np.cos(np.pi * k * x))
        cols.append(np.sin(np.pi * k * x))
    if cols:
        return np.column_stack(cols)
    return np.zeros((int(n), 1), dtype=np.float64)


def local_adam_basis(n, M, theta0, budget_s, rng, base_lr=0.18, eta_start=3.0, eta_end=100.0):
    n = int(n)
    M = int(M)
    if n < 8 or M <= 0:
        return make_cos_p(n, 0.5), 2.0
    if (not np.isfinite(budget_s)) or budget_s <= 1.0:
        p0 = normalize_nonness(softmax_from_log(build_basis(n, M) @ np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64)))
        return p0, 0.0

    B = build_basis(n, M)
    D = B.shape[1]

    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if theta.size < D:
        theta_new = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(theta.size, D)
        theta_new[:m] = theta[:m]
        theta = theta_new
    else:
        theta = theta[:D].copy()
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -5.0, 5.0)

    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    phi0 = B @ theta
    p0 = normalize_nonzero(softmax_from_log(phi0))
    sc0, _ = score_of_p(p0, L)
    best = p0.copy()
    best_score = float(sc0)

    mom = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    vel = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lr = base_lr
    fail = 0
    no_improve = 0
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + float(budget_s)
    last_best_time = start_time
    last_print = start_time

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        phi = B @ theta
        p = normalize_nonzero(softmax_from_log(phi))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            theta = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = base_lr
            continue

        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        C = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
        m = float(C.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * m)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = float(score)
            best = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            last_best_time = time.monotonic()
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start_time) / max(1.0, float(budget_s)))
        eta = eta_start + (eta_end - eta_start) * progress
        F, q = soft_F_and_q(C, m, eta)

        Q = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
        gp = np.fft.irfft(P.conj() * Q, L)[:n]
        gp = np.maximum(gp, 0.0)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gp)):
            gp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        pg = p * gp
        dotp = float(np.dot(p, gp))
        Ep = B.T @ p
        grad = B.T @ pg - dotp * Ep
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))

        if gmax <= 1e-300:
            theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, D)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -5.0, 5.0)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            fail = 0
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        t += 1
        cb1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
        cb2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t
        mom = b1 * mom + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        vel = b2 * vel + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)

        update = -lr * (mom / cb1) / (np.sqrt(vel / cb2) + 1e-8)
        update = np.where(np.isfinite(update), update, 0.0)
        mu = float(np.max(np.abs(update)))
        max_step = 1.0
        if mu > max_step:
            update *= max_step / mu

        accepted = False
        ftol = 1e-11 * max(1.0, abs(F))
        score_tol = max(0.02, 0.05 * score)

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
            th = theta + mult * update
            th = np.clip(th, -6.0, 6.0)
            pt = normalize_nonzero(softmax_from_log(B @ th))
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(pt)):
                continue
            st, Ft = trial_score_F(pt, L, eta)
            if (not np.isfinite(st)) or (not np.isfinite(Ft)):
                continue
            if Ft <= F + ftol and st <= score + score_tol:
                if (Ft < F - ftol) or (st < best_score - 1e-12):
                    theta = th
                    accepted = True
                    fail = 0
                    break

        if not accepted:
            lr = max(lr * 0.72, base_lr * 0.04)
            fail += 1
            if fail >= 10:
                if best is not None and rng.random() < 0.65:
                    zb = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
                    zb = zb - float(np.mean(zb))
                    try:
                        th2, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(B, zb, rcond=None)
                        theta = np.clip(np.asarray(th2, dtype=np.float64), -6.0, 6.0)
                    except Exception:
                        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, D)
                else:
                    theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, D)
                theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
                mom.fill(0.0)
                vel.fill(0.0)
                t = 0
                lr = base_lr
                fail = 0
                no_improve = 0

        if no_improve >= 200:
            theta += rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, D)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            no_improve = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_best_time > max(120.0, 0.25 * float(budget_s)):
            theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, D)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
            mom.fill(0.0)
            vel.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            lr = base_lr
            no_improve = 0
            last_best_time = time.monotonic()

        if time.monotonic() - last_print >= 45.0:
            print("local_basis n=%d D=%d score=%.6f lr=%.4f" % (n, D, best_score, lr), flush=True)
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def lp_improve(p0, budget_s, rng):
    p = normalize_nonzero(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n < 8:
        sc, _ = score_of_p(p)
        return p, sc
    if (not np.isfinite(budget_s)) or budget_s <= 2.0:
        sc, _ = score_of_p(p)
        return p, sc

    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
        sc, _ = score_of_p(p)
        return p, sc

    sc0, _ = score_of_p(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = float(sc0)
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + float(budget_s)
    stall = 0
    last_print = start_time
    tlist = (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)

    rows = 2 * n - 1
    K = np.arange(rows, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1, 1)
    I = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64).reshape(1, -1)
    J = K - I
    mask = (J >= 0) & (J < n)
    Jsaf = np.where(mask, J, 0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        C = convolution_fft(p)
        m = float(C.max())
        if (not np.isfinite(m)) or m <= 0.0:
            break

        A = np.where(mask, p[Jsaf], 0.0)
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c=-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=m * np.ones(rows, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s <= 1.0 + 1e-8:
            break
        gn = g / s

        improved = False
        for tt in tlist:
            pn = (1.0 - tt) * p + tt * gn
            pn = normalize_nonzero(pn)
            sc, _ = score_of_p(pn)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = pn
                best_score = float(sc)
                p = pn
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 18:
                p = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, n))
                p = normalize_nonzero(p)
                stall = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_print >= 30.0:
            print("lp_improve n=%d score=%.6f" % (n, best_score), flush=True)
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best, best_score


def score_item(item):
    return item[0]


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """Multi-scale softmax mirror search for a low 2*n*max(a*a)/sum(a)^2 sequence."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 101)
    total_budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    reserve = 15.0
    deadline = start_time + max(30.0, total_budget) - reserve

    final_n = 4096

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def _maybe_update(pp, ss):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if pp is None:
            return
        try:
            ss = float(ss)
        except Exception:
            return
        if not np.isfinite(ss):
            return
        pp = normalize_nonzero(pp)
        if ss < best_score:
            best_seq = pp.copy()
            best_score = ss

    if total_budget <= 90.0:
        n_quick = 1024
        p_quick = make_cos_p(n_quick, 0.6)
        p_quick, s_quick = local_adam(p_quick, max(3.0, total_budget - 8.0), rng, base_lr=0.25)
        _maybe_update(p_quick, s_quick)
        if best_seq is None:
            best_seq = make_cos_p(n_quick, 0.6)
        out = normalize_nonzero(best_seq)
        if time.monotonic() + 2.0 < deadline:
            try:
                es = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out]))
                if np.isfinite(es):
                    print("evaluate_sequence quick best = %.9f" % es, flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return [float(x) for x in out]

    # Optional short LP based moves on a relatively small length.
    if total_budget >= 500.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 200.0:
        n_lp = 64
        lp_budget = min(70.0, 0.035 * total_budget)
        lp_inits = []
        lp_inits.append(make_cos_p(n_lp, 0.7))
        lp_inits.append(make_cos_p(n_lp, -0.7))
        lp_inits.append(make_smooth_random_p(n_lp, rng, scale=0.8))
        if best_seq is not None:
            lp_inits.insert(0, resample_log(best_seq, n_lp))
        per_lp = max(2.0, lp_budget / max(1, len(lp_inits)))
        for p_init in lp_inits:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 200.0:
                break
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            alloc = min(per_lp, deadline - 200.0 - t0)
            if alloc < 3.0:
                continue
            p_out, s_out = lp_improve(p_init, alloc, rng)
            _maybe_update(p_out, s_out)

    # Fourier-log basis optimization at medium resolution.
    if total_budget >= 500.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
        n_basis = 1024
        M_basis = 12
        D_basis = 2 * M_basis
        basis_budget = min(430.0, 0.22 * total_budget)
        if basis_budget < 30.0:
            basis_budget = 30.0
        thetas = []
        thetas.append(np.zeros(D_basis, dtype=np.float64))
        t1 = np.zeros(D_basis, dtype=np.float64); t1[0] = 0.8; thetas.append(t1)
        t2 = np.zeros(D_basis, dtype=np.float64); t2[0] = -0.8; thetas.append(t2)
        t3 = np.zeros(D_basis, dtype=np.float64)
        if D_basis > 2:
            t3[2] = 0.45
        t3[0] = 0.35
        thetas.append(t3)
        thetas.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.42, D_basis))
        per_basis = max(3.0, basis_budget / max(1, len(thetas)))
        for th in thetas:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 300.0:
                break
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            alloc = min(per_basis, deadline - 300.0 - t0)
            if alloc < 3.0:
                continue
            p_out, s_out = local_adam_basis(n_basis, M_basis, th, alloc, rng, base_lr=0.18)
            _maybe_update(p_out, s_out)

    # If no useful thing has been found, create a baseline.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_cos_p(max(512, min(final_n, int(total_budget))), 0.55)
        scb, _ = score_of_p(best_seq)
        best_score = float(scb)

    # Final full-resolution candidates.
    cand_p = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        if best_seq.size == final_n:
            cand_p.append(best_seq.copy())
        else:
            cand_p.append(resample_log(best_seq, final_n))

    try:
        prev = height_sequence_1
        if prev is not None and len(prev) > 0:
            cand_p.append(resample_log(np.asarray(prev, dtype=np.float64), final_n))
    except Exception:
        pass

    cand_p.append(make_cos_p(final_n, 0.65))
    cand_p.append(make_cos_p(final_n, -0.65))
    cand_p.append(make_smooth_random_p(final_n, rng, scale=1.0))

    scored_cands = []
    for p in cand_p[:6]:
        p = normalize_nonzero(p)
        if p.size != final_n:
            p = resample_log(p, final_n)
        sc, _ = score_of_p(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored_cands.append((sc, p))

    if scored_cands:
        scored_cands.sort(key=score_item)
        scored_cands = scored_cands[:4]

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - now - 8.0)
    candidate_time = remaining * 0.78
    polish_time = remaining * 0.22

    if scored_cands and candidate_time > 30.0:
        per = candidate_time / float(len(scored_cands))
        for item in scored_cands:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
                break
            p_init = item[1]
            alloc = min(per, max(3.0, deadline - 20.0 - time.monotonic()))
            if alloc < 25.0:
                continue
            p_out, s_out = local_adam(p_init, alloc, rng, base_lr=0.25)
            _maybe_update(p_out, s_out)

    # Polish the best actual sequence.  If the best object is much shorter,
    # also try its final-N interpolation.
    if best_seq is not None:
        p_in = best_seq
        if p_in.size != final_n:
            p_in_final = resample_log(p_in, final_n)
            sc_raw, _ = score_of_p(p_in)
            sc_fin, _ = score_of_p(p_in_final)
            if np.isfinite(sc_raw) and np.isfinite(sc_fin):
                # Prefer the one that currently looks better, but not a tiny gap.
                if sc_fin < sc_raw * 0.999:
                    p_in = p_in_final
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            p_out, s_out = local_adam(p_in, max(3.0, deadline - 25.0 - time.monotonic()), rng, base_lr=0.25)
            _maybe_update(p_out, s_out)

    # Final validity / actual-evaluator check for the two most promising objects.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() + 6.0 < deadline:
        candidates = [normalize_nonzero(best_seq)]
        if best_seq.size != final_n:
            candidates.append(resample_log(best_seq, final_n))

        chosen = candidates[0]
        chosen_score = float(best_score)
        for cand in candidates:
            try:
                es = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in cand]))
                if np.isfinite(es) and es < chosen_score:
                    chosen_score = es
                    chosen = cand
            except Exception:
                pass

        best_seq = chosen
        best_score = chosen_score
        sc_internal, _ = score_of_p(chosen)
        print("final internal score=%.9f length=%d" % (sc_internal, chosen.size), flush=True)
        print("final best_score=%.9f" % best_score, flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_cos_p(final_n, 0.6)

    out = normalize_nonzero(best_seq)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
